^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2732)             slot_empty
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2733)             or more_explicit
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2734)             or (same_source and not auto_would_downgrade_explicit)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2735)         )
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2736) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2737)         if is_winner:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2738)             if self.expose_value:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2739)                 ctx.params[self.name] = value
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2740)                 ctx._param_default_explicit[self.name] = self._default_explicit
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2741)         elif existing_source is not None:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2742)             # Lost arbitration; restore the winning option's source.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2743)             ctx.set_parameter_source(self.name, existing_source)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2744)         # else: ctx.params[self.name] was populated by code that bypassed
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2745)         # handle_parse_result (from another option's callback for example). Keep
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2746)         # the provisional source recorded before process_value so downstream
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2747)         # lookups don't return ``None``.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2748) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2749)         return value, args

^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2791) class Option(Parameter):
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2792)     """Options are usually optional values on the command line and
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2793)     have some extra features that arguments don't have.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2794) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2795)     All other parameters are passed onwards to the parameter constructor.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2796) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2797)     :param show_default: Show the default value for this option in its
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2798)         help text. Values are not shown by default, unless
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2799)         :attr:`Context.show_default` is ``True``. If this value is a
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2800)         string, it shows that string in parentheses instead of the
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2801)         actual value. This is particularly useful for dynamic options.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2802)         For single option boolean flags, the default remains hidden if
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2803)         its value is ``False``.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2804)     :param show_envvar: Controls if an environment variable should be
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2805)         shown on the help page and error messages.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2806)         Normally, environment variables are not shown.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2807)     :param prompt: If set to ``True`` or a non empty string then the
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2808)         user will be prompted for input. If set to ``True`` the prompt
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2809)         will be the option name capitalized. A deprecated option cannot be
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2810)         prompted.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2811)     :param confirmation_prompt: Prompt a second time to confirm the
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2812)         value if it was prompted for. Can be set to a string instead of
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2813)         ``True`` to customize the message.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2814)     :param prompt_required: If set to ``False``, the user will be
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2815)         prompted for input only when the option was specified as a flag
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2816)         without a value.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2817)     :param hide_input: If this is ``True`` then the input on the prompt
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2818)         will be hidden from the user. This is useful for password input.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2819)     :param is_flag: forces this option to act as a flag.  The default is
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2820)                     auto detection.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2821)     :param flag_value: which value should be used for this flag if it's
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2822)                        enabled.  This is set to a boolean automatically if
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2823)                        the option string contains a slash to mark two options.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2824)     :param multiple: if this is set to `True` then the argument is accepted
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2825)                      multiple times and recorded.  This is similar to ``nargs``
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2826)                      in how it works but supports arbitrary number of
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2827)                      arguments.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2828)     :param count: this flag makes an option increment an integer.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2829)     :param allow_from_autoenv: if this is enabled then the value of this
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2830)                                parameter will be pulled from an environment
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2831)                                variable in case a prefix is defined on the
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2832)                                context.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2833)     :param help: the help string.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2834)     :param hidden: hide this option from help outputs.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2835)     :param attrs: Other command arguments described in :class:`Parameter`.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2836) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2837)     .. versionchanged:: 8.4.0
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2838)         Non-basic ``flag_value`` types (not ``str``, ``int``, ``float``, or
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2839)         ``bool``) are passed through unchanged instead of being stringified.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2840)         Previously, ``type=click.UNPROCESSED`` was required to preserve them.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2841) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2842)     .. versionchanged:: 8.2
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2843)         ``envvar`` used with ``flag_value`` will always use the ``flag_value``,
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2844)         previously it would use the value of the environment variable.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2845) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2846)     .. versionchanged:: 8.1

^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2930) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2931)         # The _flag_needs_value property tells the parser that this option is a flag
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2932)         # that cannot be used standalone and needs a value. With this information, the
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2933)         # parser can determine whether to consider the next user-provided argument in
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2934)         # the CLI as a value for this flag or as a new option.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2935)         # If prompt is enabled but not required, then it opens the possibility for the
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2936)         # option to gets its value from the user.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2937)         self._flag_needs_value = self.prompt is not None and not self.prompt_required
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2938) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2939)         # Auto-detect if this is a flag or not.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2940)         if is_flag is None:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2941)             # Implicitly a flag because flag_value was set.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2942)             if flag_value is not UNSET:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2943)                 is_flag = True
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2944)             # Not a flag, but when used as a flag it shows a prompt.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2945)             elif self._flag_needs_value:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2946)                 is_flag = False
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2947)             # Implicitly a flag because secondary options names were given.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2948)             elif self.secondary_opts:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2949)                 is_flag = True
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2950) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2951)         # The option is explicitly not a flag, but to determine whether or not it needs
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2952)         # value, we need to check if `flag_value` or `default` was set. Either one is
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2953)         # sufficient.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2954)         # Ref: https://github.com/pallets/click/issues/3084
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2955)         elif is_flag is False and not self._flag_needs_value:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2956)             self._flag_needs_value = flag_value is not UNSET or self.default is UNSET
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2957) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2958)         if is_flag:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2959)             # Set missing default for flags if not explicitly required or prompted.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2960)             if self.default is UNSET and not self.required and not self.prompt:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2961)                 if multiple:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2962)                     self.default = ()
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2963) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2964)             # Auto-detect the type of the flag based on the flag_value.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2965)             if type is None:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2966)                 # A flag without a flag_value is a boolean flag.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2967)                 if flag_value is UNSET:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2968)                     self.type: types.ParamType[t.Any] = types.BoolParamType()
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2969)                 # If the flag value is a boolean, use BoolParamType.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2970)                 elif isinstance(flag_value, bool):
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2971)                     self.type = types.BoolParamType()
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2972)                 # Otherwise, guess the type from the flag value.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2973)                 else:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2974)                     guessed = types.convert_type(None, flag_value)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2975)                     if (
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2976)                         isinstance(guessed, types.StringParamType)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2977)                         and not isinstance(flag_value, str)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2978)                         and flag_value is not None
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2979)                     ):
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2980)                         # The flag_value type couldn't be auto-detected
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2981)                         # (not str, int, float, or bool). Since flag_value
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2982)                         # is a programmer-provided Python object, not CLI
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2983)                         # input, pass it through unchanged instead of
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2984)                         # stringifying it.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2985)                         self.type = types.UNPROCESSED
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2986)                     else:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2987)                         self.type = guessed
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2988) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2989)         self.is_flag = bool(is_flag)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2990)         self.is_bool_flag = self.is_flag and isinstance(self.type, types.BoolParamType)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2991)         self.flag_value = flag_value
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2992) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2993)         # Set boolean flag default to False if unset and not required.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2994)         if self.is_bool_flag:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2995)             if self.default is UNSET and not self.required:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2996)                 self.default = False
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2997) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2998)         # The alignment of default to the flag_value is resolved lazily in
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2999)         # get_default() to prevent callable flag_values (like classes) from
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3000)         # being instantiated. Refs:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3001)         # https://github.com/pallets/click/issues/3121
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3002)         # https://github.com/pallets/click/issues/3024#issuecomment-3146199461
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3003)         # https://github.com/pallets/click/pull/3030/commits/06847da
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3004) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3005)         # Set the default flag_value if it is not set.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3006)         if self.flag_value is UNSET:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3007)             if self.is_flag:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3008)                 self.flag_value = True
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3009)             else:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3010)                 self.flag_value = None
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3011) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3012)         # Counting.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3013)         self.count = count
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3014)         if count:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3015)             if type is None:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3016)                 self.type = types.IntRange(min=0)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3017)             if self.default is UNSET:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3018)                 self.default = 0
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3019) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3020)         self.allow_from_autoenv = allow_from_autoenv

^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3024)         self.show_envvar = show_envvar

^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3026)         if __debug__:

^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3029) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3030)             if self.nargs == -1:

^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3032) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3033)             if not self.is_bool_flag and self.secondary_opts:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3034)                 raise TypeError("Secondary flag is not valid for non-boolean flag.")

^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3044)

^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3048)     def to_info_dict(self) -> dict[str, t.Any]:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3049)         """
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3050)         .. versionchanged:: 8.3.0
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3051)             Returns ``None`` for the :attr:`flag_value` if it was not set.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3052)         """
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3053)         info_dict = super().to_info_dict()
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3054)         info_dict.update(
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3055)             help=self.help,
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3056)             prompt=self.prompt,
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3057)             is_flag=self.is_flag,
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3058)             # We explicitly hide the :attr:`UNSET` value to the user, as we choose to
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3059)             # make it an implementation detail. And because ``to_info_dict`` has been
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3060)             # designed for documentation purposes, we return ``None`` instead.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3061)             flag_value=self.flag_value if self.flag_value is not UNSET else None,
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3062)             count=self.count,

^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3067)     def get_default(
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3068)         self, ctx: Context, call: bool = True
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3069)     ) -> t.Any | t.Callable[[], t.Any] | None:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3070)         """Return the default value for this option.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3071) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3072)         For non-boolean flag options, ``default=True`` is treated as a sentinel
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3073)         meaning "activate this flag by default" and is resolved to
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3074)         :attr:`flag_value`.  For example, with ``--upper/--lower`` feature
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3075)         switches where ``flag_value="upper"`` and ``default=True``, the default
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3076)         resolves to ``"upper"``.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3077) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3078)         .. caution::
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3079)             This substitution only applies to non-boolean flags
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3080)             (:attr:`is_bool_flag` is ``False``). For boolean flags, ``True`` is
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3081)             a legitimate Python value and ``default=True`` is returned as-is.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3082) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3083)         .. versionchanged:: 8.3.3
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3084)             ``default=True`` is no longer substituted with ``flag_value`` for
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3085)             boolean flags, fixing negative boolean flags like
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3086)             ``flag_value=False, default=True``.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3087)         """
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3088)         value = super().get_default(ctx, call=False)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3089) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3090)         # Resolve default=True to flag_value lazily (here instead of
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3091)         # __init__) to prevent callable flag_values (like classes) from
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3092)         # being instantiated by the callable check below.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3093)         if value is True and self.is_flag and not self.is_bool_flag:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3094)             value = self.flag_value
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3095)         elif call and callable(value):
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3096)             value = value()
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3097) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3098)         return value
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3099)

^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3290)         elif ctx.show_default is not None:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3291)             show_default = ctx.show_default
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3292) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3293)         if show_default_is_str or (
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3294)             show_default and (default_value not in (None, UNSET))

^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3296)             if show_default_is_str:

^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3298)             elif isinstance(default_value, (list, tuple)):
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3299)                 default_string = ", ".join(str(d) for d in default_value)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3300)             elif isinstance(default_value, enum.Enum):
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3301)                 default_string = default_value.name
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3302)             elif inspect.isfunction(default_value):
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3303)                 default_string = _("(dynamic)")
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3304)             elif self.is_bool_flag and self.secondary_opts:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3305)                 # For boolean flags that have distinct True/False opts,

^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3307)                 default_string = _split_opt(
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3308)                     (self.opts if default_value else self.secondary_opts)[0]
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3309)                 )[1]
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3310)             elif self.is_bool_flag and not self.secondary_opts and not default_value:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3311)                 default_string = ""
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3312)             elif isinstance(default_value, str) and default_value == "":
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3313)                 default_string = '""'
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3314)             else:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3315)                 default_string = str(default_value)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3316) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3317)             if default_string:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3318)                 extra["default"] = default_string
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3319) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3320)         if (
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3321)             isinstance(self.type, types._NumberRangeBase)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3322)             # skip count with default range type
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3323)             and not (self.count and self.type.min == 0 and self.type.max is None)

^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3335)     def prompt_for_value(self, ctx: Context) -> t.Any:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3336)         """This is an alternative flow that can be activated in the full
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3337)         value processing if a value does not exist.  It will prompt the
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3338)         user until a valid value exists and then returns the processed
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3339)         value as result.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3340)         """
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3341)         assert self.prompt is not None
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3342) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3343)         # Calculate the default before prompting anything to lock in the value before
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3344)         # attempting any user interaction.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3345)         default = self.get_default(ctx)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3346) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3347)         # A boolean flag can use a simplified [y/n] confirmation prompt.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3348)         if self.is_bool_flag:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3349)             # If we have no boolean default, we force the user to explicitly provide
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3350)             # one.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3351)             if default in (UNSET, None):
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3352)                 default = None
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3353)             # Nothing prevent you to declare an option that is simultaneously:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3354)             # 1) auto-detected as a boolean flag,
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3355)             # 2) allowed to prompt, and
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3356)             # 3) still declare a non-boolean default.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3357)             # This forced casting into a boolean is necessary to align any non-boolean
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3358)             # default to the prompt, which is going to be a [y/n]-style confirmation
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3359)             # because the option is still a boolean flag. That way, instead of [y/n],
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3360)             # we get [Y/n] or [y/N] depending on the truthy value of the default.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3361)             # Refs: https://github.com/pallets/click/pull/3030#discussion_r2289180249
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3362)             else:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3363)                 default = bool(default)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3364)             return confirm(self.prompt, default)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3365) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3366)         # If show_default is given, provide this to `prompt` as well,
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3367)         # otherwise we use `prompt`'s default behavior
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3368)         prompt_kwargs: t.Any = {}
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3369)         if self.show_default is not None:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3370)             prompt_kwargs["show_default"] = self.show_default
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3371) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3372)         return prompt(
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3373)             self.prompt,
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3374)             # Use ``None`` to inform the prompt() function to reiterate until a valid
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3375)             # value is provided by the user if we have no default.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3376)             default=None if default is UNSET else default,
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3377)             type=self.type,
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3378)             hide_input=self.hide_input,
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3379)             show_choices=self.show_choices,
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3380)             confirmation_prompt=self.confirmation_prompt,
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3381)             value_proc=lambda x: self.process_value(ctx, x),
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3382)             **prompt_kwargs,
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3383)         )
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3384) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3385)     def resolve_envvar_value(self, ctx: Context) -> str | None:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3386)         """:class:`Option` resolves its environment variable the same way as
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3387)         :func:`Parameter.resolve_envvar_value`, but it also supports
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3388)         :attr:`Context.auto_envvar_prefix`. If we could not find an environment from
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3389)         the :attr:`envvar` property, we fallback on :attr:`Context.auto_envvar_prefix`
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3390)         to build dynamiccaly the environment variable name using the
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3391)         :python:`{ctx.auto_envvar_prefix}_{self.name.upper()}` template.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3392) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3393)         :meta private:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3394)         """
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3395)         rv = super().resolve_envvar_value(ctx)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3396) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3397)         if rv is not None:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3398)             return rv
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3399) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3400)         if self.allow_from_autoenv and ctx.auto_envvar_prefix is not None and self.name:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3401)             envvar = f"{ctx.auto_envvar_prefix}_{self.name.upper()}"
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3402)             rv = os.environ.get(envvar)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3403) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3404)             if rv:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3405)                 return rv
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3406) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3407)         return None
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3408) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3409)     def value_from_envvar(self, ctx: Context) -> t.Any:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3410)         """For :class:`Option`, this method processes the raw environment variable
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3411)         string the same way as :func:`Parameter.value_from_envvar` does.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3412) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3413)         But in the case of non-boolean flags, the value is analyzed to determine if the
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3414)         flag is activated or not, and returns a boolean of its activation, or the
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3415)         :attr:`flag_value` if the latter is set.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3416) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3417)         This method also takes care of repeated options (i.e. options with
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3418)         :attr:`multiple` set to ``True``).
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3419) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3420)         :meta private:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3421)         """
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3422)         rv = self.resolve_envvar_value(ctx)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3423) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3424)         # Absent environment variable or an empty string is interpreted as unset.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3425)         if rv is None:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3426)             return None
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3427) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3428)         # Non-boolean flags are more liberal in what they accept. But a flag being a
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3429)         # flag, its envvar value still needs to be analyzed to determine if the flag is
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3430)         # activated or not.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3431)         if self.is_flag and not self.is_bool_flag:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3432)             # If the flag_value is set and match the envvar value, return it
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3433)             # directly.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3434)             if self.flag_value is not UNSET and rv == self.flag_value:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3435)                 return self.flag_value
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3436)             # Analyze the envvar value as a boolean to know if the flag is
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3437)             # activated or not.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3438)             return types.BoolParamType.str_to_bool(rv)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3439) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3440)         # Split the envvar value if it is allowed to be repeated.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3441)         value_depth = (self.nargs != 1) + bool(self.multiple)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3442)         if value_depth > 0:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3443)             multi_rv = self.type.split_envvar_value(rv)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3444)             if self.multiple and self.nargs != 1:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3445)                 multi_rv = batch(multi_rv, self.nargs)  # type: ignore[assignment]
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3446) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3447)             return multi_rv
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3448) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3449)         return rv
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3450) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3451)     def consume_value(
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3452)         self, ctx: Context, opts: cabc.Mapping[str, Parameter]
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3453)     ) -> tuple[t.Any, ParameterSource]:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3454)         """For :class:`Option`, the value can be collected from an interactive prompt
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3455)         if the option is a flag that needs a value (and the :attr:`prompt` property is
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3456)         set).
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3457) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3458)         Additionally, this method handles flag option that are activated without a
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3459)         value, in which case the :attr:`flag_value` is returned.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3460) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3461)         :meta private:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3462)         """
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3463)         value, source = super().consume_value(ctx, opts)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3464) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3465)         # The parser will emit a sentinel value if the option is allowed to as a flag
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3466)         # without a value.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3467)         if value is FLAG_NEEDS_VALUE:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3468)             # If the option allows for a prompt, we start an interaction with the user.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3469)             if self.prompt is not None and not ctx.resilient_parsing:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3470)                 value = self.prompt_for_value(ctx)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3471)                 source = ParameterSource.PROMPT
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3472)             # Else the flag takes its flag_value as value.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3473)             else:
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3474)                 value = self.flag_value
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3475)                 source = ParameterSource.COMMANDLINE
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3476) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3477)         # A flag which is activated always returns the flag value, unless the value
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3478)         # comes from the explicitly sets default.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3479)         elif (
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3480)             self.is_flag
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3481)             and value is True
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3482)             and not self.is_bool_flag
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3483)             and source < ParameterSource.DEFAULT_MAP
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3484)         ):
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3485)             value = self.flag_value
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3486) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3487)         # Re-interpret a multiple option which has been sent as-is by the parser.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3488)         # Here we replace each occurrence of value-less flags (marked by the
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3489)         # FLAG_NEEDS_VALUE sentinel) with the flag_value.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3490)         elif (
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3491)             self.multiple
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3492)             and value is not UNSET
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3493)             and isinstance(value, cabc.Iterable)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3494)             and source < ParameterSource.DEFAULT_MAP
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3495)             and any(v is FLAG_NEEDS_VALUE for v in value)
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3496)         ):
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3497)             value = [self.flag_value if v is FLAG_NEEDS_VALUE else v for v in value]
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3498)             source = ParameterSource.COMMANDLINE
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3499) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3500)         # The value wasn't set, or used the param's default, prompt for one to the user
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3501)         # if prompting is enabled.

^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3531) class Argument(Parameter):
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3532)     """Arguments are positional parameters to a command.  They generally
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3533)     provide fewer features than options but can have infinite ``nargs``
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3534)     and are required by default.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3535) 
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3536)     All parameters are passed onwards to the constructor of :class:`Parameter`.
^c7c87e5 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 3537)     """
